        $('#hamburger-menu a, #hamburger-menu button').on('click', function() {
            var label = $(this).attr('aria-label');

            if (!label) {
                label = $(this).text();
            }

            if (!label) {
                label = $(this).attr('href');
            }

            ga('send', 'event', 'hamburger-menu', 'click', label, { transport: 'beacon' });
        });
    });
</script>

        hitCallback: function() {
            if (window.history.replaceState && window.location.href.indexOf('utm') !== -1) {
                var params = window.location.search.substring(1).split('&');
                var newParams = params.filter(function(p) { return !p.match(/^utm/); });

                if (newParams.length) {
                    window.history.replaceState({}, '', window.location.pathname + '?' + newParams.join('&'));

                }
            }
